{operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[15:0]};\n      end\n\n      ALU_PCKHI: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8)\n          shuffle_result = {operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0], operand_c_i[15:0]};\n        else\n          shuffle_result = {operand_a_i[31:16], operand_b_i[31:16]};\n      end\n\n      ALU_SHUF: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++)\n            shuffle_result[k*8 +: 8] = operand_a_i[operand_b_i[k*8 +: 8][1:0]*8 +: 8];\n        end else begin\n          for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++)\n            shuffle_result[k*16 +: 16] = operand_a_i[operand_b_i[k*16 +: 16][0]*16 +: 16];\n        end\n      end\n\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          for (int k = +: 8] = operand_c_i[operand_b_i[k*8 +: 8][1:0]*8 +: 8];\n        end else begin\n          for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++)\n            if (operand_b_i[k*16 +: 16][1])\n              shuffle_result[k*16 +: 16] = operand_a_i[operand_b_i[k*16 +: 16][0]*16 +: 16];\n            else\n              shuffle_result[k*16 +: 16] = operand_c_i[operand_b_i[k*16 +: 16][0]*16 +: 16];\n        end\n      end\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Min/max and ABS\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] minmax_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    minmax_result = '0;\n    unique case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        bit sgn;\n        sgn = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MAX);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin\n          logic [31:0] av, bv;\n          bit    lt, gt;\n          av = {24'b0, operand_a_i[i*8 +: 8]};\n          bv = {24'b0, operand_b_i[i*8 +: 8]};\n          lt = cmp_lane(av, bv, sgn ?      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin\n          logic [7:0] av;\n          logic signed [31:0] se;\n          av = operand_a_i[i*8 +: 8];\n          se = ($signed(operand_a_i) < 0) abs_result = -operand_a_i;\n        else                          abs_result = operand_a_i;\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // CLIP\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    logic signed [31:0] a_s, b_s, lo_s;\n    a_s = $signed(operand_a_i);\n    b_s = $signed(operand_b_i);\n    lo_s = $signed(~operand_b_i);\n    clip_result = '0;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin\n      if (a_s <= 0)        clip_result = 32'h0;\n      else if (a_s < b_s)  clip_result = operand_a_i;\n      else                 clip_result = operand_b_i;\n    end else begin\n      if ((a_s < lo_s) ||  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Divider\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  logic        div_valid;\n  logic        div_signed;\n  logic        div_op_a_signed;\n  logic [ 5:0] div_shift;\n\n  assign div_signed = operator_i[0];\n  assign div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & == ALU_DIVU) ||\n                                 (operator_i == ALU_REM) || (operator_i == ALU_REMU));\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI      (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI     (rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (operand_a_i << (ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result)), // not actually used by divider? kept valid\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(cnt_result == 0),\n      .OpBSign_SI  (div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI   (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO      (result_div),\n      .InVld_SI    (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI   (ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO   (div_ready)\n  );\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Final result mux\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  always_comb begin\n    result_o = '0;\n    comparison_result_o = 1'b0;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_AND: result_o = operand_a_i & operand_b_i;\n      ALU_OR:  result_o = operand_a_i | operand_b_i;\n      ALU_XOR: result_o = operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i;\n\n      ALU_ADD, ALU_ADDR, ALU_ADDU, ALU_ADDUR,\n      ALU_SUB, ALU_SUBR, ALU_SUBU, ALU_SUBUR:\n        result_o = adder_shift_result;\n\n      ALU_SLL, ALU_SRL, ALU_SRA, ALU_ROR:\n        result_o = shift_ops_result;\n\n      ALU_BINS, ALU_BEXT, ALU_BEXTU: result_o = bextins_result;\n      ALU_BCLR: result_o = bclr_result;\n      ALU_BSET: result_o = bset_result;\n      ALU_BREV: result_o = reverse_result;\n\n      ALU_SHUF, ALU_SHUF2, ALU_PCKLO, ALU_PCKHI,\n      ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS, ALU_INS:\n        result_o = shuffle_result;\n\n      ALU_MIN, ALU_MINU, ALU_MAX, ALU_MAXU:\n        result_o = minmax_result;\n\n      ALU_ABS: result_o = abs_result;\n      ALU_CLIP, ALU_CLIPU: result_o = clip_result;\n\n      ALU_EQ, ALU_NE, ALU_GTU, ALU_GEU, ALU_LTU, ALU_LEU,\n      ALU_GTS, ALU_GES, ALU_LTS, ALU_LES: begin\n        result_o = { {8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}},\n                     {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}} };\n        comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n      end\n\n      ALU_SLTS, ALU_SLTU, ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU: begin\n        comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n        result_o = {31'b0, comparison_result_o};\n      end\n\n      ALU_FF1, ALU_FL1, ALU_CNT, ALU_CLB: begin\n        logic [5:0] bitop;\n        unique case (operator_i)\n          ALU_FF1: bitop = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n          ALU_FL1: bitop = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n          ALU_CNT: bitop = cnt_result;\n          ALU_CLB: begin\n            if (ff_no_one) bitop = operand_a_i[31] ? 6'd31 : 6'd0;\n            else           bitop = clb_result;\n          end\n          default: bitop = 6'd0;\n        endcase\n        result_o = {26'b0, bitop[5:0]};\n      end\n\n      ALU_DIV, ALU_DIVU, ALU_REM, ALU_REMU:\n        result_o = result_div;\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\nendmodule\n\n//----------------------------------------------\n// Original bit-counting and divider submodules\n//----------------------------------------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [5:0]  result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [7:0][2:0]  cnt_l2;\n  logic [3:0][3:0]  cnt_l3;\n  logic [1:0][4:0]  cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule cv32e40p_ff_one #(\n    parameter LEN = 32\n) (\n    input  logic [LEN-1:0] in_i,\n    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0] first_one_o,\n    output logic                   no_ones_o\n);\n  localparam NUM_LEVELS = $clog2(LEN);\n\n  logic [          LEN-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_lut;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0]                 sel_nodes;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n  generate\n    genvar j;\n    for (j = 0; j < LEN; j++) begin : gen_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[j] = $unsigned(j);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    genvar k;\n    genvar l;\n    genvar level;\n\n    assign sel_nodes[2**NUM_LEVELS-1] = 1'b0;\n\n    for (level = 0; level < NUM_LEVELS; level++) begin : gen_tree\n      if (level < NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_non_root_level\n        for (l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : gen_node\n          assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+l] = sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] | sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n          assign index_nodes[2**level-1+l] = (sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] == 1'b1) ?\n                                             index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] :\n                                             index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n        end\n      end\n      if (level == NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_root_level\n        for (k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : gen_node\n          if (k * 2 < LEN - 1) begin : gen_two\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k] = in_i[k*2] | in_i[k*2+1];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = (in_i[k*2] == 1'b1) ? index_lut[k*2] : index_lut[k*2+1];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 == LEN - 1) begin : gen_one\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = in_i[k*2];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = index_lut[k*2];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 > LEN - 1) begin : gen_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign first_one_o = index_nodes[0];\n  assign no_ones_o   = ~sel_nodes[0];\nendmodule\n\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {IDLE, DIVIDE, FINISH} State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ?   localparam logic [6:0] ALU_DIVU = 7'b0110000;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_DIV = 7'b0110001;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_REMU = 7'b0110010;
  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_REM = 7'b0110011;
  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE32 = 2'b00;
  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE16 = 2'b10;
  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE8 = 2'b11;

  //------------------------------------------------------------
  // Width-aware helpers
  //------------------------------------------------------------
  function automatic [31:0] sign_ext(input [31:0] v, input [1:0] w);
    unique case (w)
      2'b10: return {{16{v[15]}}, v[15:0]};
      2'b11: return {{24{v[7]}}, v[7:0]};
      default: return v;
    endcase
  endfunction

  function automatic bit cmp_lane(input [31:0] aa, input [31:0] bb,
                                  input [6:0] op, input [1:0] w);
    logic signed [31:0] as, bs;
    as = $signed(sign_ext(aa, w));
    bs = $signed(sign_ext(bb, w));
    unique case (op)
      ALU_EQ:   return aa == bb;
      ALU_NE:   return aa != bb;
      ALU_LTU, ALU_SLTU: return aa < bb;
      ALU_LTS, ALU_SLTS: return as < bs;
      ALU_LEU, ALU_SLETU: return aa <= bb;
      ALU_LES, ALU_SLETS: return as <= bs;
      ALU_GTU:  return aa > bb;
      ALU_GTS:  return as > bs;
      ALU_GEU:  return aa >= bb;
      ALU_GES:  return as >= bs;
      default:  return 1'b0;
    endcase
  endfunction

  function automatic [7:0] shl8(input [7:0] v, input [2:0] sh);

[Step 18/60]
    logic [31:0] m, v;
    draw(m); m = m % 10;
    draw(v);
    case (m)
      0: return 32'd0;  1: return 32'd1;  2: return 32'd7;  3: return 32'd8;  4: return 32'd15;  5: return 32'd16;
      6: return 32'd30; 7: return 32'd31; 8: return v % 32;
      default: return v;                          // amounts beyond 31: only the low bits count per lane
    endcase
  endfunction
  // a value of exactly `bits` significant bits (1..32), random below the leading one
  function automatic logic [31:0] with_bits(input int bits);
    logic [31:0] lead = 32'h1 << (bits - 1);
    logic [31:0] low;
    draw(low);
    return lead | (low & (lead - 1));
  endfunction

  // ---- bookkeeping --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  int total_checks, total_errors, shown, total_cycles, div_cycles;
  bit aborted;

  task automatic note_error(input string kind, input string src, input int idx, input string detail);
    total_errors++;
    if (shown < MAX_SHOWN) begin
      shown++;
      $display("%s %s#%0d op=%02h vm=%0h bma=%02h bmb=%02h ive=%0h cpx=%0h sbr=%0h csh=%0h a=%08h b=%08h c=%08h : %s",
               kind, src, idx, op, vm, bma, bmb, ive, cpx, sbr, csh, a, b, c, detail);
    end
    if (total_errors >= 1000 && !aborted) begin
      aborted = 1'b1;
      $display("TB_ABORT: 1000 errors, the remaining stimulus is skipped");
    end
  endtask

  // apply the operation held in the input signals; compare the handshake every cycle and the outputs when
  // the reference is ready. have_exp: also check the reference against a recorded expectation (phase 1).
  task automatic apply_op(input string src, input int idx, input bit have_exp, input logic [31:0] exp_res, input logic exp_c);
    int cycles; bit lat_bad; string d;
    @(negedge clk);
    if (ready_o !== ready_ref) begin
      d = $sformatf("ready_o=%b in the idle cycle, reference %b", ready_o, ready_ref);
      note_error("TB_EQUIV_ERROR", src, idx, d);
    end
    enable_i = 1'b1;
    cycles = 0; lat_bad = 1'b0;
    do begin
      @(negedge clk);
      cycles++;
      if (!lat_bad && ready_o !== ready_ref) begin
        lat_bad = 1'b1;
        d = $sformatf("ready_o=%b at poll cycle %0d, reference %b (handshake timing differs)", ready_o, cycles, ready_ref);
        note_error("TB_EQUIV_ERROR", src, idx, d);
      end
    end while (ready_ref !== 1'b1 && cycles < 64);
    total_cycles += cycles;
    if (is_div(op)) div_cycles += cycles;
    total_checks++;
    if (ready_ref !== 1'b1) begin
      note_error("TB_HARNESS_ERROR", src, idx, "the reference did not assert ready_o within 64 cycles");
    end else begin
      if (result_o !== result_ref || (is_cmp(op) && cmp_o !== cmp_ref)) begin
        d = $sformatf("expected result=%08h cmp=%s got result=%08h cmp=%b", result_ref,
                      is_cmp(op) ? $sformatf("%b", cmp_ref) : "x", result_o, cmp_o);
        note_error("TB_ERROR", src, idx, d);
      end
      if (have_exp && (result_ref !== exp_res || (is_cmp(op) && cmp_ref !== exp_c))) begin
        d = $sformatf("reference result=%08h cmp=%b differs from the recorded expectation %08h cmp=%b", result_ref, cmp_ref, exp_res, exp_c);
        note_error("TB_HARNESS_ERROR", src, idx, d);
      end
    end
    enable_i = 1'b0;
  endtask

  // one random operation within the driven envelope
  task automatic random_op();
    logic [31:0] r0, r1, m;
    logic [6:0] o;
    int nvm;
    draw(r0); r0 = r0 % 57; o = OPS[r0]; nvm = vm_choices(o);
    op = o;
    draw(r1); r1 = r1 % 3;
    vm = (nvm == 1 || (nvm == 3 && r1 == 0)) ? 2'b00 : (r1 == 1) ? 2'b10 : 2'b11;
    bma = '0; bmb = '0; ive = '0; cpx = 1'b0; sbr = 1'b0; csh = '0;
    a = rnd32(); b = rnd32(); c = rnd32();
    if (is_addfam(o)) begin
      draw(m); m = m % 100;
      if (is_rounding(o) || m < 40) begin draw(r0); bma = r0[4:0]; draw(r1); bmb = r1[4:0]; end
      if (o == OP_SUB && vm == 2'b10 && m >= 40 && m < 55) sbr = 1'b1;                       // pv.subrotmj
      else if (!is_rounding(o) && vm != 2'b11 && m >= 55 && m < 70) begin cpx = 1'b1; draw(r0); csh = r0[1:0]; bma = '0; bmb = '0; end
    end
    if (is_bitmanip(o)) begin
      draw(r0); bma = r0[4:0]; draw(r1); bmb = r1[4:0];
      draw(m); m = m % 10;
      if (o != 7'h2b && o != 7'h2c && o != 7'h49 && m < 7) b = {27'd0, bmb};                 // BEXT/BEXTU/BINS: b = bmb as the decoder does
      if (o == 7'h49) begin draw(r0); r0 = r0 % 32; b = (m < 5) ? r0 : {27'd0, bmb}; end              // BREV: left shift by b[4:0]
    end
    if (is_shift(o) && vm == 2'b00) b = shift_amt32();
    if (o == OP_EXT || o == OP_EXTS || o == OP_INS) begin draw(r0); ive = r0[1:0]; end
    if (o == OP_EXT || o == OP_EXTS) c = '0;
    if (o == OP_ABS) begin b = '0; draw(m); m = m % 5; if (vm == 2'b10 && m == 0) cpx = 1'b1; end   // complex conjugate on 16-bit lanes

shift_ops_result[23:16] = shl8(operand_a_i[23:16], operand_b_i[18:16]);\n            shift_ops_result[15: 8] = shl8(operand_a_i[15: 8], operand_b_i[10: 8]);\n            shift_ops_result[ 7: 0] = shl8(operand_a_i[ 7: 0], operand_b_i[ 2: 0]);\n          end\n          ALU_SRL, ALU_SRA, ALU_ROR: begin\n            shift_ops_result[31:24] = shr8(operand_a_i[31:24], operand_b_i[26:24], (operator_i == ALU_SRA));\n            shift_ops_result[23:16] = shr8(operand_a_i[23:16], operand_b_i[18:16], (operator_i == ALU_SRA));\n            shift_ops_result[15: 8] = shr8(operand_a_i[15: 8], operand_b_i[10: 8], (operator_i == ALU_SRA));\n            shift_ops_result[ 7: 0] = shr8(operand_a_i[ 7: 0], operand_b_i[ 2: 0], (operator_i == ALU_SRA));\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n      default: begin\n        unique case (operator_i)\n          ALU_SLL: shift_ops_result = operand_a_i << operand_b_i[4:0];\n          ALU_SRL: shift_ops_result = operand_a_i >> operand_b_i[4:0];\n          ALU_SRA: shift_ops_result = $signed(operand_a_i) >>> operand_b_i[4:0];\n          ALU_ROR: shift_ops_result = ror32_f(operand_a_i, operand_b_i[4:0]);\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Bit manipulation\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] bextins_result, bclr_result, bset_result, reverse_result;\n  always_comb begin : bitmanip\n    logic [31:0] t;\n    logic        sign_bit;\n    logic [31:0] shifted;\n\n    // BEXT / BEXTU / BINS\n    if (operator_i == ALU_BEXT) begin\n      shifted  = $signed(operand_a_i) >>> operand_b_i[4:0];\n      sign_bit = shifted[bmask_a_i];\n      bextins_result = (shifted & bmask) | ({32{sign_bit}} & bmask_inv);\n    end else if (operator_i == ALU_BEXTU) begin\n      shifted  = operand_a_i >> operand_b_i[4:0];\n      bextins_result = shifted & bmask;\n    end else begin // BINS\n      shifted  = operand_a_i << operand_b_i[4:0];\n      bextins_result = (shifted & bmask) | (operand_c_i & bmask_inv);\n    end\n\n    bclr_result = operand_a_i & bmask_inv;\n    bset_result = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n    // BREV (uses left-shifted operand)\n    t = operand_a_i << operand_b_i[4:0];\n    reverse_result = '0;\n    unique case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b01: begin\n        for (int j = 0; j < 16; j++)\n          reverse_result[2*j +: 2] = t[31 - 2*j -: 2];\n      end\n      2'b10: begin\n        for (int j = 0; j < 10; j++)\n          reverse_result[3*j +: 3] = t[31 - 3*j -: 3];\n        reverse_result[31:30] = 2'b00;\n      end\n      default: begin\n        for (int j = 0; j < 32; j++)\n          reverse_result[j] = t[31-j];\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Shuffle/pack/extract/insert\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_result;\n  always_comb begin : shuffle_unit\n    shuffle_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS: begin\n        logic [7:0] bsel;\n        logic [31:0] extval;\n        bit   is_signed;\n        is_signed = (operator_i == ALU_EXTS);\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          bsel = imm_vec_ext_i[1:0];\n          if (is_signed)\n            extval = { {24{operand_a_i[bsel*8 + 7]}}, operand_a_i[bsel*8 +: 8] };\n          else\n            extval = { 24'b0, operand_a_i[bsel*8 +: 8] };\n        end else begin\n          bsel = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b0};\n          if (is_signed)\n            extval = { {16{operand_a_i[bsel*8 + 15]}}, operand_a_i[bsel*8 +: 16] };\n          else\n            extval = { 16'b0, operand_a_i[bsel*8 +: 16] };\n        end\n        shuffle_result = extval;\n      end\n\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        shuffle_result = operand_c_i;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8)\n          shuffle_result[imm_vec_ext_i*8 +: 8] = operand_a_i[7:0];\n        else\n          shuffle_result[{imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b0}*8 +: 16] = operand_a_i[15:0];\n      end\n\n      ALU_PCKLO: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8)\n          shuffle_result = {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0]};\n        else\n          shuffle_result = {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[15:0]};\n      end\n\n      ALU_PCKHI: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8)\n          shuffle_result = {operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0], operand_c_i[15:0]};\n        else\n          shuffle_result = {operand_a_i[31:16], operand_b_i[31:16]};\n      end\n\n      ALU_SHUF: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++)\n            shuffle_result[k*8 +: 8] = operand_a_i[operand_b_i[k*8 + 1: k*8] * 8 +: 8];\n        end else begin\n          for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++)\n            shuffle_result[k*16 +: 16] = operand_a_i[operand_b_i[k*16] * 16 +: 16];\n        end\n      end\n\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++)\n            if (operand_b_i[k*8 + 2])\n              shuffle_result[k*8 +: 8] = operand_a_i[operand_b_i[k*8 + 1: k*8] * 8 +: 8];\n            else\n              shuffle_result[k*8 +: 8] = operand_c_i[operand_b_i[k*8 + 1: k*8] * 8 +: 8];\n        end else begin\n          for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++)\n            if (operand_b_i[k*16 + 1])\n              shuffle_result[k*16 +: 16] = operand_a_i[operand_b_i[k*16] * 16 +: 16];\n            else\n              shuffle_result[k*16 +: 16] = operand_c_i[operand_b_i[k*16] * 16 +: 16];\n        end\n      end\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Min/max and ABS\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] minmax_result;\n  always_comb begin : minmax_unit\n    minmax_result = '0;\n    unique case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        bit sgn;\n        sgn = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MAX);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin\n          logic [31:0] av, bv;\n          bit    lt, gt;\n          av = {24'b0, operand_a_i[i*8 +: 8]};\n          bv = {24'b0, operand_b_i[i*8 +: 8]};\n          lt = cmp_lane(av, bv, sgn ? ALU_LTS : ALU_LTU, 2'b11);\n          gt = cmp_lane(av, bv, sgn ? ALU_GTS : ALU_GTU, 2'b11);\n          if ((operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU))\n            minmax_result[i*8 +: 8] = lt ? av[7:0] : bv[7:0];\n          else\n            minmax_result[i*8 +: 8] = gt ? av[7:0] : bv[7:0];\n        end\n      end\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        bit sgn;\n        sgn = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MAX);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin\n          logic [31:0] av, bv;\n          bit    lt, gt;\n          av = {16'b0, operand_a_i[i*16 +: 16]};\n          bv = {16'b0, operand_b_i[i*16 +: 16]};\n          lt = cmp_lane(av, bv, sgn ? ALU_LTS : ALU_LTU, 2'b10);\n          gt = cmp_lane(av, bv, sgn ? ALU_GTS : ALU_GTU, 2'b10);\n          if ((operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU))\n            minmax_result[i*16 +: 16] = lt ? av[15:0] : bv[15:0];\n          else\n            minmax_result[i*16 +: 16] = gt ? av[15:0] : bv[15:0];\n        end\n      end\n      default: begin\n        bit sgn, lt, gt;\n        sgn = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MAX);\n        lt = cmp_lane(operand_a_i, operand_b_i, sgn ? ALU_LTS : ALU_LTU, 2'b00);\n        gt = cmp_lane(operand_a_i, operand_b_i, sgn ? ALU_GTS : ALU_GTU, 2'b00);\n        if ((operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU))\n          minmax_result = lt ? operand_a_i : operand_b_i;\n        else\n          minmax_result = gt ? operand_a_i : operand_b_i;\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [31:0] abs_result;\n  always_comb begin : abs_unit\n    abs_result = '0;\n    unique case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin\n          logic [15:0] av;\n          if (av[15]) abs_result[i*16 +: 16] = -av;\n          else        abs_result[i*16 +: 16] = av;\n        end\n        if (is_clpx_i)\n          abs_result = {16'h0 - operand_a_i[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]};\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin\n          logic [7:0] av;\n          if (av[7]) abs_result[i*8 +: 8] = -av;\n          else        abs_result[i*8 +: 8] = av;\n        end\n      end\n      default: begin\n        if ($signed(operand_a_i) < 0) abs_result = -operand_a_i;\n        else                          abs_result = operand_a_i;\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // CLIP\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  always_comb begin : clip_unit\n    logic signed [31:0] a_s, b_s, lo_s;\n    a_s = $signed(operand_a_i);\n    b_s = $signed(operand_b_i);\n    lo_s = $signed(~operand_b_i);\n    clip_result = '0;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin\n      if (a_s <= 0)        clip_result = 32'h0;\n      else if (a_s < b_s)  clip_result = operand_a_i;\n      else                 clip_result = operand_b_i;\n    end else begin\n      if ((a_s < lo_s) || (a_s == lo_s)) clip_result = ~operand_b_i;\n      else if (a_s > b_s)                clip_result = operand_b_i;\n      else                               clip_result = operand_a_i;\n    end\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Divider\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  logic        div_valid;\n  logic        div_signed;\n  logic        div_op_a_signed;\n  logic [ 5:0] div_shift;\n  logic [31:0] div_op_b;\n  logic [63:0] div_pre;\n\n  assign div_signed = operator_i[0];\n  assign div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & div_signed;\n  assign div_shift = (ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result) +\n                     (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  assign div_valid = enable_i & ((operator_i == ALU_DIV) || (operator_i == ALU_DIVU) ||\n                                 (operator_i == ALU_REM) || (operator_i == ALU_REMU));\n\n  // Reproduce the original shared-shifter OpB input exactly:\n  // shift_left_result = bit_rev( (zero_ext(operand_a_rev)) >> div_shift )\n  assign div_pre = {32'b0, operand_a_rev} >> div_shift[4:0];\n  genvar divb;\n  generate\n    for (divb = 0; divb < 32; divb++) begin : gen_div_opb\n      assign div_op_b[divb] = div_pre[31-divb];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI      (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI     (rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (div_op_b),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(cnt_result == 0),\n      .OpBSign_SI  (div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI   (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO      (result_div),\n      .InVld_SI    (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI   (ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO   (div_ready)\n  );\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Final result mux\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  always_comb begin : result_mux\n    result_o = '0;\n    comparison_result_o = 1'b0;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_AND: result_o = operand_a_i & operand_b_i;\n      ALU_OR:  result_o = operand_a_i | operand_b_i;\n      ALU_XOR: result_o = operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i;\n\n      ALU_ADD, ALU_ADDR, ALU_ADDU, ALU_ADDUR,\n      ALU_SUB, ALU_SUBR, ALU_SUBU, ALU_SUBUR:\n        result_o = adder_shift_result;\n\n      ALU_SLL, ALU_SRL, ALU_SRA, ALU_ROR:\n        result_o = shift_ops_result;\n\n      ALU_BINS, ALU_BEXT, ALU_BEXTU: result_o = bextins_result;\n      ALU_BCLR: result_o = bclr_result;\n      ALU_BSET: result_o = bset_result;\n      ALU_BREV: result_o = reverse_result;\n\n      ALU_SHUF, ALU_SHUF2, ALU_PCKLO, ALU_PCKHI,\n      ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS, ALU_INS:\n        result_o = shuffle_result;\n\n      ALU_MIN, ALU_MINU, ALU_MAX, ALU_MAXU:\n        result_o = minmax_result;\n\n      ALU_ABS: result_o = abs_result;\n      ALU_CLIP, ALU_CLIPU: result_o = clip_result;\n\n      ALU_EQ, ALU_NE, ALU_GTU, ALU_GEU, ALU_LTU, ALU_LEU,\n      ALU_GTS, ALU_GES, ALU_LTS, ALU_LES: begin\n        result_o = { {8{cmp_result[3]}}, {8{cmp_result[2]}},\n                     {8{cmp_result[1]}}, {8{cmp_result[0]}} };\n        comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n      end\n\n      ALU_SLTS, ALU_SLTU, ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU: begin\n        comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n        result_o = {31'b0, comparison_result_o};\n      end\n\n      ALU_FF1, ALU_FL1, ALU_CNT, ALU_CLB: begin\n        logic [5:0] bitop;\n        unique case (operator_i)\n          ALU_FF1: bitop = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_result};\n          ALU_FL1: bitop = ff_no_one ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_result};\n          ALU_CNT: bitop = cnt_result;\n          ALU_CLB: begin\n            if (ff_no_one) bitop = operand_a_i[31] ? 6'd31 : 6'd0;\n            else           bitop = clb_result;\n          end\n          default: bitop = 6'd0;\n        endcase\n        result_o = {26'b0, bitop[5:0]};\n      end\n\n      ALU_DIV, ALU_DIVU, ALU_REM, ALU_REMU:\n        result_o = result_div;\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\nendmodule\n\n//----------------------------------------------\n// Original bit-counting and divider submodules\n//----------------------------------------------\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [5:0]  result_o\n);\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [7:0][2:0]  cnt_l2;\n  logic [3:0][3:0]  cnt_l3;\n  logic [1:0][4:0]  cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule cv32e40p_ff_one #(\n    parameter LEN = 32\n) (\n    input  logic [LEN-1:0] in_i,\n    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0] first_one_o,\n    output logic                   no_ones_o\n);\n  localparam NUM_LEVELS = $clog2(LEN);\n\n  logic [          LEN-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_lut;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0]                 sel_nodes;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n  generate\n    genvar j;\n    for (j = 0; j < LEN; j++) begin : gen_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[j] = $unsigned(j);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    genvar k;\n    genvar l;\n    genvar level;\n\n    assign sel_nodes[2**NUM_LEVELS-1] = 1'b0;\n\n    for (level = 0; level < NUM_LEVELS; level++) begin : gen_tree\n      if (level < NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_non_root_level\n        for (l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : gen_node\n          assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+l] = sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] | sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n          assign index_nodes[2**level-1+l] = (sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] == 1'b1) ?\n                                             index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] :\n                                             index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n        end\n      end\n      if (level == NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_root_level\n        for (k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : gen_node\n          if (k * 2 < LEN - 1) begin : gen_two\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k] = in_i[k*2] | in_i[k*2+1];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = (in_i[k*2] == 1'b1) ? index_lut[k*2] : index_lut[k*2+1];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 == LEN - 1) begin : gen_one\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = in_i[k*2];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = index_lut[k*2];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 > LEN - 1) begin : gen_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign first_one_o = index_nodes[0];\n  assign no_ones_o   = ~sel_nodes[0];\nendmodule\n\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {IDLE, DIVIDE, FINISH} State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n  assign ABComp_S = ((AReg_DP == BReg_DP) | ((AReg_DP > BReg_DP) ^ CompInv_SP)) &\n                    ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  assign AddOut_D = (PmSel_S) ? AddTmp_D + AddMux_D : AddTmp_D - $signed(AddMux_D);\n\n  assign Cnt_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBShift_DI : ((~CntZero_S) ?   function automatic bit cmp_lane(input [31:0] aa, input [31:0] bb,
                                  input [6:0] op, input [1:0] w);
    logic signed [31:0] as, bs;
    as = $signed(sign_ext(aa, w));
    bs = $signed(sign_ext(bb, w));
    unique case (op)
      ALU_EQ:   return aa == bb;
      ALU_NE:   return aa != bb;
      ALU_LTU, ALU_SLTU: return aa < bb;
      ALU_LTS, ALU_SLTS: return as < bs;
      ALU_LEU, ALU_SLETU: return aa <= bb;
      ALU_LES, ALU_SLETS: return as <= bs;
      ALU_GTU:  return aa > bb;
      ALU_GTS:  return as > bs;
      ALU_GEU:  return aa >= bb;
      ALU_GES:  return as >= bs;
      default:  return 1'b0;
    endcase
  endfunction

  function automatic [7:0] shl8(input [7:0] v, input [2:0] sh);
    return v << sh;
  endfunction

        lt = cmp_lane(operand_a_i, operand_b_i, sgn ? ALU_LTS : ALU_LTU, 2'b00);
        gt = cmp_lane(operand_a_i, operand_b_i, sgn ? ALU_GTS : ALU_GTU, 2'b00);
        if ((operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU))
          minmax_result = lt ? operand_a_i : operand_b_i;
        else
          minmax_result = gt ? operand_a_i : operand_b_i;
      end
    endcase
  end

[Step 34/60]
  //------------------------------------------------------------
  function automatic [31:0] sign_ext(input [31:0] v, input [1:0] w);
    logic [31:0] ext;
    begin
      ext = v;
      if (w == 2'b10) begin
        if (v[15]) ext = v | 32'hFFFF0000;
        else       ext = v & 32'h0000FFFF;
      end else if (w == 2'b11) begin
        if (v[7]) ext = v | 32'hFFFFFF00;
        else      ext = v & 32'h000000FF;
      end
      return ext;
    end
  endfunction

  function automatic bit cmp_lane(input [31:0] aa, input [31:0] bb,
                                  input [6:0] op, input [1:0] w);
    logic signed [31:0] as, bs;
    as = $signed(sign_ext(aa, w));
    bs = $signed(sign_ext(bb, w));
    unique case (op)
      ALU_EQ:   return aa == bb;
      ALU_NE:   return aa != bb;
      ALU_LTU, ALU_SLTU: return aa < bb;
      ALU_LTS, ALU_SLTS: return as < bs;
      ALU_LEU, ALU_SLETU: return aa <= bb;
      ALU_LES, ALU_SLETS: return as <= bs;
      ALU_GTU:  return aa > bb;
      ALU_GTS:  return as > bs;
      ALU_GEU:  return aa >= bb;
      ALU_GES:  return as >= bs;
      default:  return 1'b0;
    endcase
  endfunction

ALU_GES, ALU_GEU: return g | e;\n      ALU_LTS, ALU_LTU, ALU_SLTS, ALU_SLTU: return !(g | e);\n      ALU_LES, ALU_LEU, ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU: return !g;\n      default:  return 1'b0;\n    endcase\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic bit cmp8_flag(input [6:0] op, input [7:0] a8, input [7:0] b8, input bit sc);\n    bit e, g;\n    if (sc) begin\n      e = $signed({ {24{a8[7]}}, a8}) == $signed({ {24{b8[7]}}, b8});\n      g = $signed({ {24{a8[7]}}, a8}) >  $signed({ {24{b8[7]}}, b8});\n    end else begin\n      e = a8 == b8;\n      g = a8 > b8;\n    end\n    return cmp16_flag(op, e, g);\n  endfunction\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Adder family\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic        adder_sub;\n  logic [31:0] add_a_eff, add_b_eff;\n  logic [31:0] adder_sum;\n  logic [31:0] adder_rounding;\n  logic [31:0] adder_round_result;\n  logic        adder_arith;\n  logic [31:0] shift_amt_norm;\n  logic [15:0] clpx_shift_ex;\n  logic [31:0] adder_shift_result;\n\n  assign adder_sub = (operator_i == ALU_SUB) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR) ||\n                     (operator_i == ALU_SUBU) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR) ||\n                     is_subrot_i;\n\n  assign add_a_eff = is_subrot_i ? {operand_b_i[15:0], operand_a_i[31:16]} : operand_a_i;\n  assign add_b_eff = is_subrot_i ? {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[31:16]} : operand_b_i;\n\n  always_comb begin : adder_lanes\n    adder_sum = 32'h0;\n    unique case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        if (adder_sub)\n          adder_sum = {add_a_eff[31:16] - add_b_eff[31:16],\n                       add_a_eff[15: 0] - add_b_eff[15: 0]};\n        else\n          adder_sum = {add_a_eff[31:16] + add_b_eff[31:16],\n                       add_a_eff[15: 0] + add_b_eff[15: 0]};\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        if (adder_sub) begin\n          adder_sum[31:24] = add_a_eff[31:24] - add_b_eff[31:24];\n          adder_sum[23:16] = add_a_eff[23:16] - add_b_eff[23:16];\n          adder_sum[15: 8] = add_a_eff[15: 8] - add_b_eff[15: 8];\n          adder_sum[ 7: 0] = add_a_eff[ 7: 0] - add_b_eff[ 7: 0];\n        end else begin\n          adder_sum[31:24] = add_a_eff[31:24] + add_b_eff[31:24];\n          adder_sum[23:16] = add_a_eff[23:16] + add_b_eff[23:16];\n          adder_sum[15: 8] = add_a_eff[15: 8] + add_b_eff[15: 8];\n          adder_sum[ 7: 0] = add_a_eff[ 7: 0] + add_b_eff[ 7: 0];\n        end\n      end\n      default: begin\n        if (adder_sub) adder_sum = add_a_eff - add_b_eff;\n        else           adder_sum = add_a_eff + add_b_eff;\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign clpx_shift_ex = {14'b0, clpx_shift_i};\n  assign shift_amt_norm = is_clpx_i ? {clpx_shift_ex, clpx_shift_ex} :\n                                       {4{3'b000, bmask_b_i}};\n  assign adder_rounding = ((operator_i == ALU_ADDR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR) ||\n                           (operator_i == ALU_ADDUR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR)) ?\n                           (bmask >> 1) : 32'h0;\n  assign adder_arith = (operator_i == ALU_ADD) || (operator_i == ALU_SUB) ||\n                       (operator_i == ALU_ADDR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR);\n  assign adder_round_result = adder_sum + adder_rounding;\n\n  always_comb begin : adder_shift_block\n    logic [31:0] tmp_hi, tmp_lo;\n    logic [31:0] tmp0, tmp1, tmp2, tmp3;\n    tmp_hi = 32'h0; tmp_lo = 32'h0;\n    tmp0 = 32'h0; tmp1 = 32'h0; tmp2 = 32'h0; tmp3 = 32'h0;\n    unique case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        if (adder_arith) begin\n          tmp_hi = $signed({ {16{adder_round_result[31]}}, adder_round_result[31:16] }) >>> shift_amt_norm[19:16];\n          tmp_lo = $signed({ {16{adder_round_result[15]}}, adder_round_result[15: 0] }) >>> shift_amt_norm[3:0];\n        end else begin\n          tmp_hi = adder_round_result[31:16] >> shift_amt_norm[19:16];\n          tmp_lo = adder_round_result[15: 0] >> shift_amt_norm[3:0];\n        end\n        adder_shift_result = {tmp_hi[15:0], tmp_lo[15:0]};\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        if (adder_arith) begin\n          tmp0 = $signed({ {24{adder_round_result[ 7]}}, adder_round_result[ 7: 0] }) >>> shift_amt_norm[2:0];\n          tmp1 = $signed({ {24{adder_round_result[15]}}, adder_round_result[15: 8] }) >>> shift_amt_norm[10:8];\n          tmp2 = $signed({ {24{adder_round_result[23]}}, adder_round_result[23:16] }) >>> shift_amt_norm[18:16];\n          tmp3 = $signed({ {24{adder_round_result[31]}}, adder_round_result[31:24] }) >>> shift_amt_norm[26:24];\n        end else begin\n          tmp0 = {24'b0, adder_round_result[ 7: 0]} >> shift_amt_norm[2:0];\n          tmp1 = {24'b0, adder_round_result[15: 8]} >> shift_amt_norm[10:8];\n          tmp2 = {24'b0, adder_round_result[23:16]} >> shift_amt_norm[18:16];\n          tmp3 = {24'b0, adder_round_result[31:24]} >> shift_amt_norm[26:24];\n        end\n        adder_shift_result = {tmp3[7:0], tmp2[7:0], tmp1[7:0], tmp0[7:0]};\n      end\n      default: begin\n        if (adder_arith)\n          adder_shift_result = $signed(adder_round_result) >>> shift_amt_norm[4:0];\n        else\n          adder_shift_result = adder_round_result >> shift_amt_norm[4:0];\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Shift operations\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] shift_ops_result;\n  always_comb begin : shift_ops_block\n    logic [31:0] tmphi, tmplo, tm0, tm1, tm2, tm3;\n    shift_ops_result = 32'h0;\n    tmphi = 32'h0; tmplo = 32'h0; tm0 = 32'h0; tm1 = 32'h0; tm2 = 32'h0; tm3 = 32'h0;\n\n    unique case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        if (operator_i == ALU_SLL) begin\n          tmphi = {16'b0, operand_a_i[31:16]} << operand_b_i[19:16];\n          tmplo = {16'b0, operand_a_i[15: 0]} << operand_b_i[3:0];\n          shift_ops_result = {tmphi[15:0], tmplo[15:0]};\n        end else begin // SRL / SRA / ROR(16-bit mode degenerates to logical right)\n          if (operator_i == ALU_SRA) begin\n            tmphi = $signed({ {16{operand_a_i[31]}}, operand_a_i[31:16] }) >>> operand_b_i[19:16];\n            tmplo = $signed({ {16{operand_a_i[15]}}, operand_a_i[15: 0] }) >>> operand_b_i[3:0];\n          end else begin\n            tmphi = {16'b0, operand_a_i[31:16]} >> operand_b_i[19:16];\n            tmplo = {16'b0, operand_a_i[15: 0]} >> operand_b_i[3:0];\n          end\n          shift_ops_result = {tmphi[15:0], tmplo[15:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        if (operator_i == ALU_SLL) begin\n          tm0 = {24'b0, operand_a_i[7:0]} << operand_b_i[2:0];\n          tm1 = {24'b0, operand_a_i[15:8]} << operand_b_i[10:8];\n          tm2 = {24'b0, operand_a_i[23:16]} << operand_b_i[18:16];\n          tm3 = {24'b0, operand_a_i[31:24]} << operand_b_i[26:24];\n          shift_ops_result = {tm3[7:0], tm2[7:0], tm1[7:0], tm0[7:0]};\n        end else begin\n          if (operator_i == ALU_SRA) begin\n            tm0 = $signed({ {24{operand_a_i[7]}}, operand_a_i[7:0] }) >>> operand_b_i[2:0];\n            tm1 = $signed({ {24{operand_a_i[15]}}, operand_a_i[15:8] }) >>> operand_b_i[10:8];\n            tm2 = $signed({ {24{operand_a_i[23]}}, operand_a_i[23:16] }) >>> operand_b_i[18:16];\n            tm3 = $signed({ {24{operand_a_i[31]}}, operand_a_i[31:24] }) >>> operand_b_i[26:24];\n          end else begin\n            tm0 = {24'b0, operand_a_i[7:0]} >> operand_b_i[2:0];\n            tm1 = {24'b0, operand_a_i[15:8]} >> operand_b_i[10:8];\n            tm2 = {24'b0, operand_a_i[23:16]} >> operand_b_i[18:16];\n            tm3 = {24'b0, operand_a_i[31:24]} >> operand_b_i[26:24];\n          end\n          shift_ops_result = {tm3[7:0], tm2[7:0], tm1[7:0], tm0[7:0]};\n        end\n      end\n      default: begin\n        unique case (operator_i)\n          ALU_SLL: shift_ops_result = operand_a_i << operand_b_i[4:0];\n          ALU_SRL: shift_ops_result = operand_a_i >> operand_b_i[4:0];\n          ALU_SRA: shift_ops_result = $signed(operand_a_i) >>> operand_b_i[4:0];\n          ALU_ROR: begin\n            logic [4:0] sh;\n            sh = operand_b_i[4:0];\n            if (sh == 0) shift_ops_result = operand_a_i;\n            else shift_ops_result = (operand_a_i >> sh) |\n                                    (operand_a_i << (6'd32 - sh));\n          end\n          default: ;\n        endcase\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Bit manipulation\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] bextins_result, bclr_result, bset_result, reverse_result;\n  always_comb begin : bitman_block\n    logic [31:0] t;\n    logic [31:0] shifted;\n    logic        sign_bit;\n\n    t = 32'h0; sign_bit = 1'b0; shifted = 32'h0;\n\n    if (operator_i == ALU_BEXT) begin\n      shifted  = $signed(operand_a_i) >>> operand_b_i[4:0];\n      sign_bit = shifted[bmask_a_i];\n      bextins_result = (shifted & bmask) | ({32{sign_bit}} & bmask_inv);\n    end else if (operator_i == ALU_BEXTU) begin\n      shifted  = operand_a_i >> operand_b_i[4:0];\n      bextins_result = shifted & bmask;\n    end else begin // BINS\n      shifted  = operand_a_i << operand_b_i[4:0];\n      bextins_result = (shifted & bmask) | (operand_c_i & bmask_inv);\n    end\n\n    bclr_result = operand_a_i & bmask_inv;\n    bset_result = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n    t = operand_a_i << operand_b_i[4:0];\n    reverse_result = 32'h0;\n    unique case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n      2'b01: begin\n        for (int j = 0; j < 16; j++)\n          reverse_result[2*j +: 2] = t[31 - 2*j -: 2];\n      end\n      2'b10: begin\n        for (int j = 0; j < 10; j++)\n          reverse_result[3*j +: 3] = t[31 - 3*j -: 3];\n        reverse_result[31:30] = 2'b00;\n      end\n      default: begin\n        for (int j = 0; j < 32; j++)\n          reverse_result[j] = t[31-j];\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Shuffle/pack/extract/insert\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] shuffle_result;\n  always_comb begin : shuffle_block\n    logic [7:0] bsel;\n    logic [31:0] extval;\n    bit is_signed;\n    shuffle_result = 32'h0;\n    bsel = 8'h0; extval = 32'h0; is_signed = 1'b0;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS: begin\n        is_signed = (operator_i == ALU_EXTS);\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          bsel = imm_vec_ext_i;\n          if (is_signed)\n            extval = { {24{operand_a_i[bsel*8 + 7]}}, operand_a_i[bsel*8 +: 8] };\n          else\n            extval = { 24'b0, operand_a_i[bsel*8 +: 8] };\n        end else begin\n          bsel = {imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b0};\n          if (is_signed)\n            extval = { {16{operand_a_i[bsel*8 + 15]}}, operand_a_i[bsel*8 +: 16] };\n          else\n            extval = { 16'b0, operand_a_i[bsel*8 +: 16] };\n        end\n        shuffle_result = extval;\n      end\n\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        shuffle_result = operand_c_i;\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8)\n          shuffle_result[imm_vec_ext_i*8 +: 8] = operand_a_i[7:0];\n        else\n          shuffle_result[{imm_vec_ext_i[0], 1'b0}*8 +: 16] = operand_a_i[15:0];\n      end\n\n      ALU_PCKLO: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8)\n          shuffle_result = {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0]};\n        else\n          shuffle_result = {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[15:0]};\n      end\n\n      ALU_PCKHI: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8)\n          shuffle_result = {operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0], operand_c_i[15:0]};\n        else\n          shuffle_result = {operand_a_i[31:16], operand_b_i[31:16]};\n      end\n\n      ALU_SHUF: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) begin\n            logic [1:0] selb;\n            selb = operand_b_i[k*8 +: 2];\n            shuffle_result[k*8 +: 8] = operand_a_i[selb*8 +: 8];\n          end\n        end else begin\n          for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) begin\n            logic sel0;\n            sel0 = operand_b_i[k*16];\n            shuffle_result[k*16 +: 16] = operand_a_i[sel0*16 +: 16];\n          end\n        end\n      end\n\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) begin\n            logic [1:0] selb;\n            selb = operand_b_i[k*8 +: 2];\n            if (operand_b_i[k*8 + 2])\n              shuffle_result[k*8 +: 8] = operand_a_i[selb*8 +: 8];\n            else\n              shuffle_result[k*8 +: 8] = operand_c_i[selb*8 +: 8];\n          end\n        end else begin\n          for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) begin\n            logic sel0;\n            sel0 = operand_b_i[k*16];\n            if (operand_b_i[k*16 + 1])\n              shuffle_result[k*16 +: 16] = operand_a_i[sel0*16 +: 16];\n            else\n              shuffle_result[k*16 +: 16] = operand_c_i[sel0*16 +: 16];\n          end\n        end\n      end\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Min/max\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] minmax_result;\n  always_comb begin : minmax_block\n    logic signed [31:0] as, bs;\n    bit do_signed;\n    minmax_result = 32'h0;\n    do_signed = (operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MAX);\n\n    if (do_signed) begin\n      as = $signed(operand_a_i);\n      bs = $signed(operand_b_i);\n    end else begin\n      as = $signed(operand_a_i);\n      bs = $signed(operand_b_i); // unsigned comparisons handled below\n    end\n\n    unique case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin\n          logic [7:0] av, bv;\n          bit selected;\n          av = operand_a_i[i*8 +: 8];\n          bv = operand_b_i[i*8 +: 8];\n          if (do_signed) selected = $signed({ {24{av[7]}}, av}) < $signed({ {24{bv[7]}}, bv});\n          else           selected = av < bv;\n          if ((operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU))\n            minmax_result[i*8 +: 8] = selected ? av : bv;\n          else\n            minmax_result[i*8 +: 8] = selected ? bv : av;\n        end\n      end\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin\n          logic [15:0] av, bv;\n          bit selected;\n          av = operand_a_i[i*16 +: 16];\n          bv = operand_b_i[i*16 +: 16];\n          if (do_signed) selected = $signed({ {16{av[15]}}, av}) < $signed({ {16{bv[15]}}, bv});\n          else           selected = av < bv;\n          if ((operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU))\n            minmax_result[i*16 +: 16] = selected ? av : bv;\n          else\n            minmax_result[i*16 +: 16] = selected ? bv : av;\n        end\n      end\n      default: begin\n        bit selected;\n        if (do_signed) selected = $signed(operand_a_i) < $signed(operand_b_i);\n        else           selected = operand_a_i < operand_b_i;\n        if ((operator_i == ALU_MIN) || (operator_i == ALU_MINU))\n          minmax_result = selected ? operand_a_i : operand_b_i;\n        else\n          minmax_result = selected ? operand_b_i : operand_a_i;\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // ABS\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] abs_result;\n  always_comb begin : abs_block\n    abs_result = 32'h0;\n    unique case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin\n          logic [15:0] av;\n          av = operand_a_i[i*16 +: 16];\n          if (av[15]) abs_result[i*16 +: 16] = 16'h0 - av;\n          else        abs_result[i*16 +: 16] = av;\n        end\n        if (is_clpx_i)\n          abs_result = {16'h0 - operand_a_i[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]};\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin\n          logic [7:0] av;\n          av = operand_a_i[i*8 +: 8];\n          if (av[7]) abs_result[i*8 +: 8] = 8'h0 - av;\n          else       abs_result[i*8 +: 8] = av;\n        end\n      end\n      default: begin\n        if ($signed(operand_a_i) < 0) abs_result = 32'h0 - operand_a_i;\n        else                          abs_result = operand_a_i;\n      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // CLIP\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  always_comb begin : clip_block\n    logic signed [31:0] a_s, b_s, lo_s;\n    a_s = $signed(operand_a_i);\n    b_s = $signed(operand_b_i);\n    lo_s = $signed(~operand_b_i);\n    clip_result = 32'h0;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin\n      if (a_s <= 0)       clip_result = 32'h0;\n      else if (a_s < b_s) clip_result = operand_a_i;\n      else                clip_result = operand_b_i;\n    end else begin\n      if ((a_s < lo_s) || (a_s == lo_s)) clip_result = ~operand_b_i;\n      else if (a_s > b_s)                clip_result = operand_b_i;\n      else                               clip_result = operand_a_i;\n    end\n  end\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Divider\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  logic        div_valid;\n  logic        div_signed;\n  logic        div_op_a_signed;\n  logic [ 5:0] div_shift;\n  logic [31:0] div_op_b;\n  logic [63:0] div_pre;\n\n  assign div_signed = operator_i[0];\n  assign div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & div_signed;\n  assign div_shift = (ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result) +\n                     (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  assign div_valid = enable_i & ((operator_i == ALU_DIV) || (operator_i == ALU_DIVU) ||\n                                 (operator_i == ALU_REM) || (operator_i == ALU_REMU));\n\n  assign div_pre = {32'b0, operand_a_rev} >> div_shift[4:0];\n  genvar divb;\n  generate\n    for (divb = 0; divb < 32; divb++) begin : gen_div_opb\n      assign div_op_b[divb] = div_pre[31-divb];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI      (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI     (rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (div_op_b),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(cnt_result == 0),\n      .OpBSign_SI  (div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI   (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO      (result_div),\n      .InVld_SI    (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI   (ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO   (div_ready)\n  );\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Final result mux\n  //------------------------------------------------------------\n  always_comb begin : result_mux\n    result_o = 32'h0;\n    comparison_result_o = 1'b0;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_AND: result_o = operand_a_i & operand_b_i;\n      ALU_OR:  result_o = operand_a_i | operand_b_i;\n      ALU_XOR: result_o = operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i;\n\n      ALU_ADD, ALU_ADDR, ALU_ADDU, ALU_ADDUR,\n      ALU_SUB, ALU_SUBR, ALU_SUBU, ALU_SUBUR:\n        result_o = adder_shift_result;\n\n      ALU_SLL, ALU_SRL, ALU_SRA, ALU_ROR:\n        result_o = shift_ops_result;\n\n      ALU_BINS, ALU_BEXT, ALU_BEXTU: result_o = bextins_result;\n      ALU_BCLR: result_o = bclr_result;\n      ALU_BSET: result_o = bset_result;\n      ALU_BREV: result_o = reverse_result;\n\n      ALU_SHUF, ALU_SHUF2, ALU_PCKLO, ALU_PCKHI,\n      ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS, ALU_INS:\n        result_o = shuffle_result;\n\n      ALU_MIN, ALU_MINU, ALU_MAX, ALU_MAXU:\n        result_o = minmax_result;\n\n   // comparison flag function-like logic: use inline functions below in continuous module,
  // but implemented as always_comb with simple functions.
  function automatic bit cmp16_flag(input [6:0] op, input bit e, input bit g);
    unique case (op)
      ALU_EQ:   return e;
      ALU_NE:   return !e;
      ALU_GTS, ALU_GTU: return g;
      ALU_GES, ALU_GEU: return g | e;
      ALU_LTS, ALU_LTU, ALU_SLTS, ALU_SLTU: return !(g | e);
      ALU_LES, ALU_LEU, ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU: return !g;
      default:  return 1'b0;
    endcase
  endfunction

  function automatic bit cmp8_flag(input [6:0] op, input [7:0] a8, input [7:0] b8, input bit sc);
    bit e, g;
    if (sc) begin
      e = $signed({ {24{a8[7]}}, a8}) == $signed({ {24{b8[7]}}, b8});
      g = $signed({ {24{a8[7]}}, a8}) >  $signed({ {24{b8[7]}}, b8});
    end else begin
      e = a8 == b8;
      g = a8 > b8;
    end
    return cmp16_flag(op, e, g);
  endfunction

        // lane 0 = bits 15:0, lane 1 = bits 31:16, replicated over byte flags
        bit e0,e1, g0,g1;
        if (sc) begin
          e0 = $signed({ {16{operand_a_i[15]}}, operand_a_i[15:0]}) == $signed({ {16{operand_b_i[15]}}, operand_b_i[15:0]});
          g0 = $signed({ {16{operand_a_i[15]}}, operand_a_i[15:0]}) >  $signed({ {16{operand_b_i[15]}}, operand_b_i[15:0]});
          e1 = $signed({ {16{operand_a_i[31]}}, operand_a_i[31:16]}) == $signed({ {16{operand_b_i[31]}}, operand_b_i[31:16]});
          g1 = $signed({ {16{operand_a_i[31]}}, operand_a_i[31:16]}) >  $signed({ {16{operand_b_i[31]}}, operand_b_i[31:16]});
        end else begin
          e0 = {16'b0, operand_a_i[15:0]} == {16'b0, operand_b_i[15:0]};
          g0 = {16'b0, operand_a_i[15:0]} >  {16'b0, operand_b_i[15:0]};
          e1 = {16'b0, operand_a_i[31:16]} == {16'b0, operand_b_i[31:16]};
          g1 = {16'b0, operand_a_i[31:16]} >  {16'b0, operand_b_i[31:16]};
        end
        cr[0] = cmp16_flag(op, e0, g0);
        cr[1] = cmp16_flag(op, e0, g0);
        cr[2] = cmp16_flag(op, e1, g1);
        cr[3] = cmp16_flag(op, e1, g1);
      end
      VEC_MODE8: begin
        cr[0] = cmp8_flag(op, operand_a_i[7:0],  operand_b_i[7:0],  sc);
        cr[1] = cmp8_flag(op, operand_a_i[15:8], operand_b_i[15:8], sc);
        cr[2] = cmp8_flag(op, operand_a_i[23:16], operand_b_i[23:16], sc);
        cr[3] = cmp8_flag(op, operand_a_i[31:24], operand_b_i[31:24], sc);
      end
      default: begin
        bit e, g;
        if (sc) begin
          e = $signed(operand_a_i) == $signed(operand_b_i);
          g = $signed(operand_a_i) >  $signed(operand_b_i);
        end else begin
          e = operand_a_i == operand_b_i;
          g = operand_a_i > operand_b_i;
        end
        cr = {4{cmp16_flag(op, e, g)}};
      end
    endcase
    cmp_result = cr;
  end

  // comparison flag expression, used by cmp_block and minmax below (hand-inlined
  // with local copies because functions with replicate+indexing stress some tools).
  logic dummy_cmp; // unused, structural only
  assign dummy_cmp = 1'b0;

  // Comparison flag expression (function with no replicate/indexing pitfalls).
  function automatic bit cmp_flag_expr(input [6:0] op, input bit e, input bit g);
    return (op == ALU_EQ) ? e :
           (op == ALU_NE) ? !e :
           (op == ALU_GTS || op == ALU_GTU) ? g :
           (op == ALU_GES || op == ALU_GEU) ? (g | e) :
           (op == ALU_LTS || op == ALU_LTU || op == ALU_SLTS || op == ALU_SLTU) ? !(g | e) :
           (op == ALU_LES || op == ALU_LEU || op == ALU_SLETS || op == ALU_SLETU) ? !g :
           1'b0;
  endfunction

  // Comparison flag expression (function with no replicate/indexing pitfalls).
  function automatic bit cmp_flag_expr(input [6:0] op, input bit e, input bit g);
    begin
      cmp_flag_expr = 1'b0;
      unique case (op)
        ALU_EQ: cmp_flag_expr = e;
        ALU_NE: cmp_flag_expr = !e;
        ALU_GTS, ALU_GTU: cmp_flag_expr = g;
        ALU_GES, ALU_GEU: cmp_flag_expr = g | e;
        ALU_LTS, ALU_LTU, ALU_SLTS, ALU_SLTU: cmp_flag_expr = !(g | e);
        ALU_LES, ALU_LEU, ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU: cmp_flag_expr = !g;
        default: cmp_flag_expr = 1'b0;

[ASSISTANT]
Summary:

1. **Approaches tried**
   - Evaluated the provided `starting_point.sv` directly first. It passed all checks with area_delay_product = 645629.32 (area ~441.5, delay ~1462 ps at default target 500 ps, slack violation).
   - Explored `target_delay` variants for the baseline (`1500`, `1000`) to see cost trade-offs. `1500` met timing but cost was slightly worse (656040); `1000` did not improve over the default reported cost.
   - Attempted two restructured implementations:
     - `design_direct.sv`: direct per-family ALU logic, no shared adder/shifter.
     - `design_direct2.sv`: same approach cleaned up and with helper functions removed/inlined.

2. **What worked best**
   - The original `starting_point.sv` was the only design that achieved full correctness. Its evaluation remained the best passing result: **PASS, area_delay_product 645629.319**.

3. **What didn’t work / caused regressions**
   - `design_direct.sv`:
     - Verilog syntax/tooling issues with function return placement and replication/indexing expressions.
     - Correctness failures in ABS/CLIP/vector comparison and divider handshake timing.
     - Never reached a successful PPA evaluation.
   - `design_direct2.sv`:
     - Removed the problematic helper-function constructs, but still had vector compare lane sign-extension bugs.
     - Divider input reconstruction (`OpB_DI`) was not equivalent to the shared-shifter path; handshake timing diverged immediately for DIVU, and simulation failed.
     - Final evaluation still failed with `ready_o` mismatch on DIVU and wrong division results.

4. **Lessons learned**
   - The original shared adder/shifter/divider datapath is cycle-critical and difficult to replace without exact equivalence of the divider’s `OpB_DI` / `div_shift` logic.
   - Function syntax with returned replication or indexed part-selects is fragile across the tools. Prefer explicit continuous assignments and simple helper functions.
   - Start from the known-good file and make smaller, deliberately targeted edits, not wholesale rewrites.
   - Spending many steps on a direct rewrite that did not pass left no chance to exploit small area-delay optimizations on the passing baseline.
   - Next time would keep `starting_point.sv` identical for divider/bit-count logic and only optimize isolated combinational paths, validating each incremental edit immediately.
